We find that modern ST serves people only to a limited extent -- only around half of interactions are rated as usable -- with significant gaps in reported usability across demographic groups. Moreover, among quality metrics, we find that QA-based evaluation is a substantially stronger predictor of real-world usability than standard approaches. Together, these findings stress the importance of situated, user-centered evaluation frameworks that go beyond holistic quality scores and attend to who the technology serves -- and how well.

</details>


### [183] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用隐藏状态的 l2 范数作为大语言模型层级推理强度的内生指标，并基于该指标设计无需训练的测试时扩展方法以提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注大语言模型推理机制，但缺少一种原则化、模型内生且能刻画逐层推理动态的信号。作者希望找到可解释、可验证、可干预的指标，用于感知和控制模型内部推理过程。

Method: 作者使用稀疏自编码器作为诊断探针，观察推理特征在后层的激活模式；从潜在几何角度建立推理强度与隐藏状态 l2 范数之间的形式联系，并证明 l2 范数可约束 SAE 推理特征激活强度；进一步通过相关性分析和因果干预验证指标有效性。基于该信号提出三种测试时方法：自适应逐层推理递归、内生推理状态 steering、l2 引导响应选择。

Result: 实验显示，隐藏状态 l2 范数与关键推理步骤及推理特征激活高度一致；基于 l2 范数的三类测试时技术在不同模型架构和基准任务上显著提升推理表现，且无需额外训练或数据。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态 l2 范数可作为大语言模型推理强度的简单、原则化且可信的内生信号，并能用于测试时控制模型推理动态，从而改进推理能力。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [184] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估一种基于大语言模型的上下文问答系统，通过在 SQuAD1.1 数据集上微调预训练模型来提升答案抽取与生成质量；实验中 RoBERTa-base 表现最佳，取得 ROUGE-L 86.84%、BLEU 28.24%、BERTScore 95.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问答系统即使在给定正确上下文的情况下，仍可能生成无关、不精确或不一致的答案，尤其面对复杂或歧义问题时存在上下文理解不足、答案一致性差和跨领域泛化能力有限等问题。

Method: 以文本上下文和对应问题作为输入，输出简洁准确答案；采用监督学习方式，在 SQuAD1.1 这类高质量上下文-问题-答案三元组数据集上对预训练大语言模型进行微调，以增强模型的上下文理解与答案抽取能力，并使用 ROUGE-L、BLEU、BERTScore 等指标进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，微调后的 RoBERTa-base 模型取得最高性能：ROUGE-L 为 86.84%，BLEU 为 28.24%，BERTScore 为 95.38%，说明生成答案在相关性和语义匹配方面表现较强。

Conclusion: 针对性微调能够显著提升基于上下文的问答系统可靠性、精确性和答案相关性；该方法对提升 LLM 在问答任务中的上下文理解与答案生成能力具有有效性。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [185] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，除输入长度与关键信息位置外，词汇密度也是导致大语言模型长上下文性能下降的重要因素；在相同长度和受控位置下，信息密度越高，检索性能越容易崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将长上下文性能退化归因于上下文长度过长或相关信息位置不利，但现实输入往往是紧凑且信息丰富的。作者希望检验“词汇密度”是否会系统性缩小模型的有效上下文窗口。

Method: 作者在9B到685B规模的开源大语言模型上进行实验，使用三类类似“needle-in-a-haystack”的检索基准。各基准保持约12k tokens的相同长度，并控制needle位置，但逐步提高上下文的信息密度；同时在每个基准内部调节密度，以排除任务类型差异带来的混杂影响。

Result: 在稀疏上下文中接近满分的模型，在高词汇密度基准上检索分数会急剧下降，部分降至60%以下。降低上下文密度通常能恢复性能，尤其是在高密度导致退化明显的区域。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的有效上下文容量不仅取决于名义上下文长度和关键信息位置，也显著受词汇密度影响。对于处理压缩、信息密集型真实输入的长上下文系统，密度控制与输入组织方式具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [186] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用FiLM进行说话人条件化，将x-vector说话人信息注入冻结ASR编码器的每个Transformer层，以适配病理语音识别，同时尽量保持基座模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语音ASR已较成熟，但由神经系统疾病导致的病理语音仍难以识别；传统微调可能成本高、易破坏原模型能力，因此需要参数高效且保留泛化能力的个体化适配方法。

Method: 使用x-vector提取说话人表征，并通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation在冻结ASR编码器各Transformer层调制内部特征；在西班牙语和英语病理语音ASR任务上，与标准微调、参数高效微调及后处理方法进行基准比较；同时评估适配后模型是否仍能回答与语音相关的问题。

Result: 说话人条件化ASR在病理语音识别上与已有适配策略具有竞争力，并且在非条件化语音上仍能保持性能。

Conclusion: FiLM式说话人条件化是一种有效的病理语音ASR适配方案，可在不更新基座模型权重的情况下实现个体化建模，并较好保留原模型的通用能力。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [187] [Benchmarking Open-Source Layout Detection Models for Data Snapshot Extraction from Institutional Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06242)
*AJ Carl P. Dy,Aivin V. Solatorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布用于“数据快照抽取”的基准数据集与评测框架，发现现有开源版面检测模型难以泛化到机构运营类文档中的语义化图表抽取任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构文档中的图表包含大量可复用的运营与分析信息，但现有文档版面分析通常将图表视为普通版面对象，缺乏对其语义价值和可解释上下文的识别能力，因此难以满足实际数据复用需求。

Method: 作者构建了覆盖人道主义报告、世界银行政策研究工作论文和项目评估文件的基准数据集，标注包含可复用分析信息的图与表；同时建立评测框架，对多个开源版面检测模型进行基准测试，评估检测性能与空间抽取质量。

Result: 实验表明，现有模型虽然在传统学术文档基准上表现较强，但在机构运营类文档上泛化不足。主要错误包括混淆分析性与非分析性内容、将复合分析对象碎片化、以及未能完整抽取解释所需的上下文信息。

Conclusion: 论文指出通用文档版面分析与实际可用的数据快照抽取之间仍存在明显差距，并通过公开PDF、标注数据、元数据和代码，为后续运营文档智能研究提供资源基础。

Abstract: Institutional documents contain substantial amounts of operational and analytical information embedded within figures and tables. Current approaches for extracting visual content from documents are largely built around generic document layout analysis, where figures and tables are treated as uniformly relevant document objects rather than semantically meaningful analytical artifacts. In this work, we introduce a benchmark dataset and evaluation framework for \textit{data snapshot extraction}, the task of identifying and localizing semantically meaningful visual artifacts within institutional documents. The benchmark spans humanitarian reports, World Bank policy research working papers, and project appraisal documents, and includes annotations for figures and tables that contain reusable analytical information. Using this dataset, we benchmarked multiple open-source layout detection models and evaluated both detection performance and spatial extraction quality. Our results show that current models struggle to generalize to operational institutional documents despite strong performance on conventional academic benchmarks. Common failure modes include confusion between analytical and non-analytical content, fragmentation of composite analytical artifacts, and incomplete extraction of contextual information required for interpretation. These findings highlight a persistent gap between generic document layout analysis and operationally useful data snapshot extraction. We release the source PDFs, annotation dataset, metadata, and source code to support future research in operational document intelligence. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ai4data/data-snapshot and the source code is available at https://github.com/worldbank/ai4data/tree/main/experimental/data-snapshot.

</details>


### [188] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [189] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，电路发现中观察到的结构差异不一定代表不同机制；在相同任务、不同输入统计条件下，结构上看似专门化的电路实际上可能执行相同功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性研究常把发现电路之间的结构差异解释为模型内部机制不同。作者希望检验这一假设是否可靠，特别是在任务保持不变但输入统计分布变化时，电路结构差异是否真的对应功能差异。

Method: 作者以 Literal Sequence Copying 任务为实验对象，在四个 token 频率区间和一个控制条件下，对五个 Pythia 模型（70M 到 1.4B）进行电路抽取，共得到 75 个电路。随后通过跨频段边迁移、共享核心电路评估、因果互换干预、同频段重复抽取，以及 source-level 与 edge-level 评估对比来分析结构与功能关系。

Result: 实验发现，频段特异的边可以广泛迁移到其他频段；大多数频段共享的核心电路能恢复至少 99% 的电路性能；因果互换干预显示不同频段的内部表征可互换；同一频段内重复抽取也会得到不同但有效的子图。这说明不同结构电路可能实现相同计算。

Conclusion: 论文提出“幻影专门化”现象：电路结构差异可能只是发现算法在等价有效子图集合中采样的结果，而非真实机制差异。因此，不能仅凭结构差异断言机制不同，需要采用边级评估和跨条件迁移测试来验证功能差异。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [190] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FOXGLOVE 数据集，用于系统比较训练有素写作教师与前沿大语言模型生成的写作反馈，并分析二者在反馈目标、句子锚定、优先级和质量评价上的一致与差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺少对 LLM 写作反馈与专家反馈的系统比较，尤其是在写作修订研究认为关键的维度上：目标导向性、是否锚定到具体句子、以及反馈优先级。

Method: 研究构建 FOXGLOVE：包含 69 篇十二年级议论文上的 696 条教师反馈，以及在统一协议下由四个前沿 LLM 生成的 1,644 条反馈，总计 2,340 条评论；同时由专家对教师与 LLM 反馈的一个子集进行质量评分，并比较反馈分布、句子定位、语言复杂度、提问使用和质量维度。

Result: 教师和 LLM 在反馈目标与文章位置分布上较为相似，但在选择哪些具体句子给予反馈方面存在分歧。LLM 倾向于生成更复杂、更长的反馈，且比教师更少使用问题形式。教师评分显示，LLM 反馈在多数质量维度上得分更高，但这一优势很大程度上可能来自评论长度更长。

Conclusion: FOXGLOVE 为系统分析人类专家与 LLM 写作反馈的对齐、分歧和差异提供了数据基础；研究表明 LLM 反馈具有较高表面质量，但其优势需谨慎解释，尤其要控制长度等因素。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [191] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PropMe，用于区分大语言模型在对抗性前缀攻击下的“可被提取能力”和普通使用场景下的“泄漏倾向”，并发现二者存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化评估多关注模型是否能在特定攻击或强诱导条件下复现训练数据，但这并不等同于模型在日常、非对抗性使用中是否会自然泄漏训练数据。因此需要一种同时衡量最坏情况可提取性与普通场景泄漏倾向的评估框架。

Method: 作者提出 PropMe 框架，通过对现有记忆化度量进行倾向感知的指标变换，构造 propensity metrics；同时提出 SimpleTrace，这是一个基于 infini-gram 的轻量级追踪流程，可将模型生成内容确定性归因到大规模训练语料，并计算逐字、近逐字以及倾向变换后的记忆化指标。实验评估两个开放模型 Comma 与 DFM Decoder，在 Common Pile 与 Dynaword 两个数据集及两种语言上比较前缀攻击、通用提示和数据集特定提示。

Result: 实验显示，前缀攻击诱发的记忆化信号显著强于通用或数据集特定提示；总体上 propensity scores 较低，说明模型虽可在直接诱导下暴露训练数据，但在普通非对抗场景中较少发生。此外，DFM Decoder 由 Comma 持续预训练而来，但其在 Common Pile 上的记忆化及泄漏倾向降低，表明后续训练数据分布变化可能降低已有数据的记忆化能力。

Conclusion: 论文认为记忆化审计不应只报告最坏情况下的训练数据可提取性，还应报告普通使用场景下的泄漏倾向，以更全面地理解和评估大语言模型的记忆化风险。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [192] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将事实性谄媚分解为“真值边际”和“操纵敏感性”两个机制，并在56个开放权重模型上分析规模与指令微调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用答案翻转率衡量事实性谄媚会混淆模型本身坚持正确答案的能力与外部社会压力导致偏移的程度，因此难以解释模型规模和指令微调分别带来的影响。

Method: 作者将事实性谄媚分解为两个通道：真值边际（模型在无压力下偏好正确答案的强度）和操纵敏感性（压力使模型偏离正确答案的幅度）。实验覆盖56个0.3B至32B参数的开放权重模型，并测试13类操纵方式，以比较基础模型与指令微调模型在不同规模下的表现。

Result: 研究发现，脆弱性主要受模型规模影响，但指令微调会改变规模的作用方式：小型指令微调模型可能更不稳健，而大型指令微调模型通常更稳健。指令微调主要提高真值边际，但其行为效果取决于操纵类型。规模扩大时，基础模型真值边际增加但操纵敏感性略升；指令微调模型真值边际增长更快且敏感性下降。

Conclusion: 事实性谄媚不是单一标量属性，不能仅用翻转率评价。更合理的评估应按机制通道、操纵类型和模型规模分别报告稳健性。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [193] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文审计伦巴第语（Lombard）可用语料，发现大规模网页抓取数据质量虚高，存在语言误识别、模板文本和噪声，并且有效数据在正字法与地域变体覆盖上严重不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多低资源语言缺乏高质量数据，限制了机器翻译等NLP任务的发展。作者关注伦巴第语这一低资源语言连续体，旨在验证现有平行与单语语料是否真正可用，以及其内部变体是否被公平代表。

Method: 作者对伦巴第语的平行语料和单语语料进行人工审计，区分网页抓取数据、人工整理语料和基准数据集；同时分析有效伦巴第语文本中的正字法构成和不同变体的覆盖情况。

Result: 研究发现所谓丰富的网页抓取数据多为假象：大量样本存在语言识别错误、网页样板文本和非语言噪声。高质量语料主要偏向西部伦巴第语变体，东部变体覆盖不足；不同数据源之间还存在相互冲突的正字法体系。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖大规模抓取无法解决低资源语言数据问题。伦巴第语NLP资源建设需要面向具体变体、重视社区参与和人工质量控制的数据整理策略，而不是只追求数据规模。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [194] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EDIT，用于训练更忠实于评分细则和答案证据的大语言模型评分器，通过诊断并修正局部推理错误、再用信念引导的强化学习校准评分过程，在多学科真实评分基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的 rubric 评分不仅要预测分数准确，还需要每个判断都能被评分标准和学生答案中的证据支撑。现有 credit-assignment 与 intervention 方法多面向数学等自包含推理任务，难以定位评分推理的具体错误位置，也难以刻画模型在推理过程中对最终分数信念的变化。

Method: 提出两阶段框架 Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training（EDIT）。第一阶段 EDIT-SFT 利用模型内部信号，包括最终分数的后验信念和输入 grounding 分数，定位有问题的推理步骤，并借助 rubric checklist 仅局部改写这些步骤。第二阶段 EDIT-RL 使用 belief-guided reward shaping 对评分器进行校准，惩罚有害的大幅信念漂移，同时保留有益探索。

Result: 在两个真实世界、多学科评分基准上，EDIT 在域内和域外划分中均持续优于强监督微调和强化学习基线。消融实验表明，内部状态诊断是性能提升的关键来源。

Conclusion: EDIT 通过结合证据诊断、局部推理修正与信念引导奖励，有效提升了 LLM 评分器对评分细则的忠实性、可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [195] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言作为生成式方法，研究意识相关结构是否可在最小人类语言先验下由任务压力产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工系统意识研究主要依赖理论清单式判别评估或直接构造意识启发架构，但二者难以排除观察到的结构来自人类语言先验或人工设计偏置。

Method: 作者提出一种生成式方法：让多智能体在最小初始条件下通过强化学习和任务压力发展通信，并分析环境复杂性、涌现通信及其意识相关结构；同时在最小环境中进行概念验证实验。

Result: 实验中，智能体发展出自指性通信，并出现一种回声-不匹配检测回路；该回路并非由任务结构或架构本身直接预测，而是由特定环境可供性诱发。

Conclusion: 涌现语言可作为研究人工系统意识相关结构的生成式工具，有助于区分由任务需求因果产生的结构与由人类语言先验或架构设计引入的结构。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [196] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CollabSim，用于系统评估基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中的协作能力，而不只看最终任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统评估多关注任务结果或单智能体推理、规划、工具使用能力，但研究表明系统失败常源于协作能力不足，如建立共同理解、维持共享任务状态、协调个体与集体目标、修复交互偏差等。

Method: 作者引入 CollabSim，一个可配置仿真框架，结合基于协作理论的能力定义、对交互条件的受控操纵，以及对智能体内部状态的动作级探测，以分析多智能体协作表现。

Result: 在四种大语言模型上的实验显示，CollabSim 能捕捉不同交互条件带来的影响，区分模型间的表现模式，并揭示智能体设计在不同任务中的依赖性效果。

Conclusion: CollabSim 为评估和诊断 LLM 多智能体系统的协作能力提供了系统化工具，有助于从单纯结果评估转向对协作过程与机制的细粒度分析。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [197] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个 Komi-Yazva—Russian 平行语料库，并提出可复现的评测协议，用于研究极低资源、濒危语言场景下的大语言模型翻译能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 濒危且极低资源语言缺乏高质量平行语料和统一评测流程，导致大语言模型翻译能力难以被可靠比较；同时，小规模数据容易出现数据泄漏、指标不一致和失败样本处理不透明等问题。

Method: 作者收集并对齐了来自 74 个叙事文本的 457 个句对，记录数据来源、句级对齐和故事标识；采用故事级交叉验证避免泄漏，比较零样本与基于检索的少样本提示；使用确定性检索、输出合法性校验、参考译文指标与评审式指标，并给出故事级不确定性估计。

Result: 不同大语言模型均能生成一定质量的译文，但性能受模型家族和提示方式影响显著；检索式少样本提示稳定优于零样本，但继续增加检索上下文后的收益有限；评测结论会明显受到指标选择和失败输出处理方式影响。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅贡献了一个新的极低资源平行语料库，也提供了一个强调防泄漏、可复现和多指标评估的濒危语言机器翻译测试平台。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [198] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用基于结果奖励的强化学习训练大语言模型，使其学会利用上下文中的语言学知识来翻译完全未见过的低资源语言，并优于上下文学习和监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法可通过持续训练或在上下文中提供语法书来翻译低资源语言，但往往对特定语言过拟合，测试时对未见语言的零样本迁移能力有限。作者认为，规模化处理极低资源语言需要模型掌握“利用上下文语言知识”的元技能，而不是记忆具体语言。

Method: 作者提出一种面向未见语言翻译的强化学习方法：在提供丰富语言学上下文的条件下，让模型生成翻译，并使用表层翻译指标 chrF 作为奖励信号进行优化。该方法强调结果驱动的学习，使模型从上下文中抽取并应用相关语法、词汇或结构信息。

Result: 实验表明，即使奖励信号较轻量，经过强化学习训练的模型也能更有效地利用给定语言学上下文，在完全未见语言上的翻译质量优于普通上下文学习和监督微调方法。

Conclusion: 论文认为，基于结果的强化学习不仅适用于数学、编程等传统推理任务，也可作为模型从上下文中学习语言知识、提升未见低资源语言翻译能力的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [199] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用反事实语境修订来审计大语言模型的用户立场模拟，发现模型模拟的立场会在文本和多模态语境改写下发生有效且稳健的转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常被用于模拟社交媒体用户并预测其对线上讨论的反应，但尚不清楚这些模拟是否真正反映用户特定信念，还是会受到与语义目标相对独立的上下文变化显著影响。

Method: 研究以原始在线对话为输入，先推断目标用户对特定主题的立场；随后采用受控的反事实语境修订策略改写对话上下文，并再次模拟用户立场。方法比较了纯文本修订策略与引入 meme 语境的多模态修订策略，并使用平均方向性立场偏移和立场转移率作为主要评估指标。

Result: 实验结果显示，在不同的极化偏好机制下，文本和多模态语境修订策略都能引发有效且稳健的立场转移。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个评估 LLM 立场模拟上下文敏感性的框架，表明 LLM 在模拟线上观点动态方面具有应用潜力，同时也存在因语境操控导致模拟结果不稳定或被误用的风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [200] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NF-CoT 用 normalizing flows 在大语言模型中建模连续潜在思维，在保留自回归生成、采样、KV-cache 和似然估计优势的同时，降低推理成本并提升代码生成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式 CoT 将中间推理强制表达为离散、串行、面向沟通的文本 token，可能低效且限制语义性、不确定或未完全成形的中间计算；现有潜在推理方法虽更高带宽，但往往损失自回归 LM 中 CoT 的关键工程与概率建模优势。

Method: 提出 NF-CoT：在 LLM backbone 内嵌 TARFlow 风格的 normalizing flow。连续思维由 NF head 在因果序列中生成，文本位置仍由标准 LM head 生成；连续 thoughts 从显式 CoT 蒸馏而来，并具有精确可计算似然。

Result: 在代码生成基准上，NF-CoT 相比显式 CoT 和既有潜在推理基线提高 pass rate，同时显著减少中间推理开销。

Conclusion: NF-CoT 为潜在推理提供了兼具连续高带宽计算与自回归语言模型兼容性的框架，可实现高效、可采样、可训练且似然可估计的隐式推理。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [201] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 主动探索能显著改善成人对合取因果规则的学习，但合取规则仍比析取规则更难、需要更多测试；部分大语言模型推断准确率接近人类，但探索效率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有因果学习研究发现成人在识别需要多个原因同时存在才产生结果的合取规则时表现较差，而在析取规则下表现更好；但这些结论多来自被动观察、证据有限的范式，尚不清楚当学习者可主动生成证据时该偏差是否仍然存在。

Method: 论文采用改造版“blicket detector”任务，让成人参与者在合取或析取因果规则结构下自由进行干预测试，以识别因果物体；同时将人类表现与多种大语言模型在相同任务中的表现进行比较，考察推断准确率和探索策略效率。

Result: 主动探索显著提升了成人的合取因果推理能力；不过相较析取规则，合取规则仍需要更多测试才能被推断。部分先进大语言模型在假设推断准确率上接近人类水平，但通常采用效率较低的探索策略，并表现出类似的合取—析取性能差距。

Conclusion: 所谓“合取劣势”并非完全固定不变，学习者的主动干预能力可以缓解该困难；但合取因果结构在认知和策略层面仍更具挑战。大语言模型可在结果准确性上接近人类，却在主动探索效率和实验设计策略上仍存在不足。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [202] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），在KV共享架构中复用跨层路由索引，以降低长上下文解码开销并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM在长上下文推理中受解码效率限制，尤其是长推理链场景。现有结构化块稀疏注意力加速明显但质量损失较大；token级稀疏注意力精度较好但需要对完整KV cache做top-k路由，端到端加速有限。

Method: 基于YOCO等KV共享架构，CLSA不仅跨cross-decoder层共享KV cache，还共享路由索引。由单个indexer一次性计算token级top-k选择，并在多层中复用该索引，从而保留细粒度token选择能力，同时摊销路由计算成本。

Result: 在短上下文和长上下文基准上，CLSA兼具准确性与效率；在128K上下文下实现最高7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升。

Conclusion: CLSA提供了一种更完整的长上下文LLM架构方案，能够同时改善预填充、KV缓存存储和长上下文解码等主要推理瓶颈，在质量与效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [203] [Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474)
*Paul Jünger,Justin Lovelace,Linxi Zhao,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SARDI：利用离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中被丢弃的低置信度预测 token 作为“前瞻”检索线索，从而在输出最终确定前动态增强证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型并行生成整段文本，在每一步会预测所有掩码位置并保留高置信度 token、丢弃低置信度 token。作者观察到，这些被丢弃的低置信度 token 虽未被采纳，但往往较早暴露关键实体或语义线索，可用于提前检索更强证据，改进检索增强生成。

Method: 提出 Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models（SARDI），一种动态 RAG 框架。在扩散去噪轨迹中收集低置信度/前瞻 token，将其用于构造或指导检索查询，并把检索到的证据反馈给模型后续去噪过程。该方法无需训练、与检索器无关，并可应用于具备推理能力的离散扩散语言模型。

Result: 在五个多跳问答基准上，SARDI 优于现有免训练的扩散式与自回归式检索增强基线，并实现最高约 8 倍吞吐量优势。

Conclusion: 被扩散模型丢弃的中间预测并非噪声，而是有价值的前瞻信号。SARDI 证明了利用扩散去噪过程中的未提交 token 进行动态检索，可以在保持高效率的同时提升多跳问答性能。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.

</details>


### [204] [Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481)
*Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tianjun Yao,Xinyi Shang,Yi Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpAI-Bench，用于研究在人机协同逐步编辑过程中，AI 写作痕迹如何在不同粒度上出现、累积或消退，并发现混合作者版本往往比纯人类或高度 AI 编辑文本更难检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 文本检测基准多关注最终文本输出，难以刻画真实写作流程中人类文本经由 AI 逐步修订后的作者归属信号变化；尤其缺少对文档、句子、token 和 span 多粒度来源标注及编辑历史的系统分析。

Method: 从人类撰写文档出发，按照预设 AI 覆盖率与五类代表性 AI 编辑操作，为每个样本构建九个连续修订版本，覆盖四个领域，并保留多粒度作者来源信息；随后使用 8 个文档级、7 个句子级和 2 个 token/span 级检测器进行综合评估。

Result: 实验表明，AI 文本可检测性不仅受 AI 编辑比例影响，还受到编辑操作类型、文本领域和累积修订历史影响；混合作者的中间版本常比完全人写或高度 AI 编辑版本更难检测，呈现非单调检测模式。

Conclusion: OpAI-Bench 提供了一个受控且贴近真实渐进式编辑场景的测试平台，可用于分析 AI 辅助写作在何时、如何以及是否变得可检测，并揭示现有检测基准忽略的复杂检测规律。

Abstract: As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [A Comprehensive Survey on Semantic Communication in Non-Terrestrial Networks: Architectures, Methodologies, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05216)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述语义通信在第六代无线网络非地面网络中的应用，指出非地面网络的关键限制与语义通信能力之间存在高度互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非地面网络可扩展覆盖范围，但面临长距离路径损耗、传播时延、动态多普勒、可见窗口有限以及星载/机载能耗与算力受限等问题；语义通信通过传输任务相关语义而非原始比特，有望缓解带宽、信道质量和资源约束。

Method: 文章按非地面网络限制逐项分析对应的语义通信设计选择，并从非地面平台、语义方法和支撑技术三个维度组织已有文献；随后分别讨论卫星中心、UAV/HAPS中心以及综合SAGIN系统中的研究进展。

Result: 综述表明，语义通信的任务导向压缩、深度联合源信道编码和生成式AI重建能力可分别应对带宽稀缺、低信噪比链路退化以及雨衰/遮挡下信息缺失等非地面网络挑战。

Conclusion: 语义通信是提升非地面网络效率、鲁棒性和智能化水平的有前景方向；未来需关注基础模型、能量感知调度、标准化缺口以及面向深空通信的量子辅助语义通信等开放问题。

Abstract: The sixth-generation wireless networks are envisioned to deliver ubiquitous, seamless, and intelligent connectivity that reaches far beyond the limits of terrestrial infrastructure. Non-terrestrial networks (NTNs) are central to this vision, extending coverage to underserved regions, remote terrain, and disaster zones that terrestrial deployment cannot economically reach. However, NTN architecture faces numerous limitations: severe path loss over long distances, long propagation delays, large and time-varying Doppler shifts, limited visibility windows, and tight on-board energy and computing budgets. Semantic communication (SemCom), which conveys the meaning of data rather than its raw bit-level representation, is unusually well matched to these conditions: extreme compression rate for task-oriented eases bandwidth scarcity, deep joint source-channel coding prevents the cliff effect due to low signal-to-noise ratio, and generative-AI reconstructs content from sparse cues that survive rain-faded or blocked links. This observation, that each NTN limitation maps onto a SemCom property that addresses it, motivates our survey. We first walk through the NTN limitations one by one, pairing each with the SemCom design choices that complement it, then we organize the literature along three axes: the NTN platform, the semantic methodology, and the supporting techniques, and follow this with platform-by-platform deep dives on satellite-centric, UAV/HAPS-centric, and integrated SAGIN systems. The survey concludes by identifying open research problems, gaps in existing standards, and future directions, including the application of foundation models, energy-aware scheduling, and quantum-assisted SemCom for deep space communication.

</details>


### [206] [Bounded Deep Unfolding for Joint Beamforming and Scheduling in Multi-Cell MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05246)
*Jiansheng Li,Shuqi Chai,Fan Xu,Kaiming Shen,Guangxu Zhu,Junting Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合深度展开与解析优化先验的联合框架，用于多小区 MIMO 下行网络中的 RBG 调度与波束成形优化，以提升加权和速率并降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FastFP 框架虽能为 WSR 最大化提供可靠的模型驱动解法，但连续波束成形更新较保守，离散 RBG 匹配阶段计算开销过高，限制了实际部署效率。

Method: 提出由 P-Net 和 K-Net 组成的联合深度展开框架：P-Net 沿 FastFP 解析更新方向学习自适应松弛因子，并将其限制在保持上升性的区间内；K-Net 学习长时域优先级策略，引导低复杂度贪婪 RBG 分配以替代高复杂度 Hungarian 匹配。两者采用解析算法先验与循环参数共享。

Result: 仿真显示，该框架相比传统模型驱动基线获得更高 WSR 和更快执行速度，并能在未见过的网络规模、天线配置和信道条件下保持较强泛化能力，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将可解释的优化算法结构与学习模块结合，可在保持收敛与单调改进理论保证的同时，显著提升无线资源调度和波束成形优化的性能与效率。

Abstract: This paper investigates the joint resource block group (RBG) scheduling and beamforming optimization problem for weighted sum-rate (WSR) maximization in multi-cell multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) downlink networks. While the Fast Fractional Programming (FastFP) framework provides a reliable model-driven solution, it suffers from conservative continuous beamforming updates and prohibitive computational overhead during the discrete RBG matching phase. To address these bottlenecks, we propose a joint deep unfolding framework comprising two core modules: P-Net and K-Net. For continuous beamforming, P-Net learns an adaptive relaxation factor along the analytical FastFP update direction. By strictly constraining this factor within an ascent-preserving interval, P-Net accelerates the optimization trajectory while rigorously retaining monotonic improvement and stationary-point convergence guarantees. For discrete RBG scheduling, K-Net learns a long-horizon priority policy that guides a low-complexity greedy assignment, effectively preserving the assignment quality while bypassing the high complexity of Hungarian matching. Both networks leverage analytical algorithmic priors and utilize recurrent parameter sharing, enabling flexible inference beyond the training horizon. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed joint framework achieves higher WSR and faster execution times than conventional model-driven baselines, while generalizing robustly across unseen network scales, antenna configurations, and channel conditions without retraining.

</details>


### [207] [X-Band UAV-enabled Integrated Sensing and Communications for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05262)
*Remon Polus,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究车联网中无人机赋能的集成感知与通信（ISaC）系统的最优时间分配问题，在通信速率与感知可靠性约束下平衡感知精度和通信性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机可作为空中平台同时提供感知与通信服务，适用于智能交通系统；但实际无人机约束、信道衰落、地面目标距离等因素会导致感知与通信资源竞争，因此需要优化时间分配。

Method: 论文构建X波段UAV-ISaC车联网系统模型，考虑单阴影与双阴影信道模型，分析感知精度与通信性能之间的权衡，并建立优化框架，在最低通信速率和感知可靠性约束下分配感知与通信时间。

Result: 仿真表明，最优时间分配会随UAV到地面信道条件和目标距离自适应变化；信道质量和目标距离会显著影响感知与通信之间的资源平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过自适应时间分配可提升UAV-ISaC系统在智能交通场景中的整体性能，为车联网中兼顾感知可靠性与通信服务质量的资源管理提供了方法。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly considered as aerial platforms capable of providing both sensing and communication services, representing a promising paradigm for intelligent transportation systems. This paper investigates the optimal time allocation for a UAV-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISaC) system operating in the X-band for vehicular networks. We analyze the trade-off between sensing accuracy and communication performance under practical UAV constraints and fading effects, considering both single-shadowing and double-shadowing channel models. An optimization framework is developed to allocate time between sensing and communication while guaranteeing minimum communication rates and sufficient sensing reliability. Simulation results demonstrate adaptive time allocation strategies, highlighting how UAV-to-ground channel conditions and target distances influence the balance between sensing and communication in smart mobility scenarios.

</details>


### [208] [Unlocking Exponential and Unbounded Robust Gains in Shannon Capacity of Classical Multiple Access Channels with Causal CSIT via Quantum Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05412)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明，在具有因果信道状态信息的经典多用户接入信道中，仅向发送端提供量子纠缠辅助可带来远超以往的容量增益，且该增益可随用户数指数增长或随状态字母表规模无界增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明量子纠缠辅助可提升经典通信网络的 Shannon 容量，但此前观测到的最大提升幅度较小（低于 6%）。本文旨在回答：在经典通信网络中，量子纠缠辅助是否可能带来显著甚至巨大的容量优势。

Method: 作者研究带有因果信道状态信息的 K 用户经典多址信道，构造输入、输出和状态均为二进制固定字母表的信道族，并分析在发送端具备量子纠缠辅助与无纠缠辅助两种情况下的容量差异；同时考察固定用户数 K=3、二进制输入输出但状态字母表增长时的容量优势，并评估噪声量子资源下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在某些 K 用户多址信道中，量子纠缠辅助带来的乘性容量优势随用户数 K 指数增长；当 K=3 且输入输出保持二进制时，优势可随状态字母表规模增长而无界。具体例子中，二进制输入、输出和状态下，K=5 与 K=7 时容量乘性增益分别超过 21 和 88。即使每个纠缠量子比特以约 30% 概率完全退相干，指数级容量优势仍然存在。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在具备因果信道状态信息的多用户经典通信场景中，发送端量子纠缠辅助可产生非常大的、可扩展且对噪声具有鲁棒性的容量提升，显著突破了此前认为量子纠缠辅助增益较小的经验认识。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement assistance is known to improve the Shannon capacity of classical communication networks but the largest gains noted thus far are rather modest (less than 6%), motivating the question: are large capacity gains ever possible? It is shown in this work that in the presence of causal channel state information at the transmitters, quantum entanglement assistance provides a multiplicative capacity advantage that grows exponentially with the number of users K for certain classical K-user multiple access channels with fixed size (binary) alphabet for inputs, outputs and states. Similarly, in the presence of causal channel state information at the transmitters, quantum entanglement assistance is shown to provide a multiplicative capacity advantage that is unbounded as the size of the state alphabet grows, while the number of users (K=3) and the input and output alphabet (binary) are held fixed. Even with only a few users and small alphabet sizes, substantial multiplicative gains in capacity are found, e.g., with binary inputs, outputs and states, multiplicative gains by factors exceeding 21 and 88 are noted with K=5 and K=7 users, respectively. The gains are robust in the sense that they persist even with noisy quantum resources, e.g., an exponential (in K) capacity advantage from quantum entanglement assistance remains available even if each entangled qubit independently depolarizes completely with probability $\approx$ 30%. The gains are based on quantum entanglement assistance provided only to the transmitters.

</details>


### [209] [Sequence Reconstruction for Substitution Channel: New Sufficient Conditions and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05454)
*Chen Wang,Eitan Yaakobi,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文针对序列重构问题提出一种新的唯一重构充分条件框架，不仅考虑 noisy reads 的数量，还考虑 reads 之间的距离，并给出理论分析与高效重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Levenshtein 相关工作主要研究保证唯一重构所需的最少读取次数，这是一类基于读取数量的充分条件。作者认为仅考虑 reads 数量不够精细，因此希望从 reads 之间的结构关系出发，建立更一般或更实用的唯一重构判据。

Method: 提出新的唯一重构框架，将读取次数与 noisy reads 之间的距离信息共同纳入条件；围绕该充分条件进行理论分析，并设计对应的高效序列重构算法。

Result: 摘要表明论文获得了新的唯一重构充分条件，并提供了与该框架匹配的有效算法；但未给出具体定理形式、复杂度或实验/性能指标。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了传统序列重构研究中仅依赖 reads 数量的视角，强调 reads 间距离可用于增强唯一重构判定与算法设计。

Abstract: In the sequence reconstruction problem, a codeword $\x$ is transmitted through several identical channels where each channel produces a noisy read of $\x$, and the problem is to analyze how to uniquely reconstruct $\x$ based on these noisy reads. Levenshtein has studied the minimum number of reads which guarantees unique reconstruction of $\x$, which is one sufficient condition for unique reconstruction. In this paper, we move on to a different perspective and propose a new framework for unique reconstruction. Our new sufficient condition for unique reconstruction takes both the number of reads and the distances among the reads into consideration. We offer both theoretical analysis and corresponding efficient reconstruction algorithms for our reconstruction framework.

</details>


### [210] [Robust Repair of Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05573)
*Wilton Kim,Stanislav Kruglik,Gaojun Luo,Han Mao Kiah*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Reed–Solomon 码在低通信带宽下单个擦除节点的鲁棒修复问题，将下载的 trace 视为“修复-trace 码”，分析其纠错能力并提出高效修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Guruswami–Wootters trace 修复框架主要关注低带宽修复，但实际分布式存储中辅助节点可能返回错误信息。论文旨在回答：在存在错误 helper 响应时，失败节点能否仍被正确修复，以及在给定错误数下原码维度应满足什么条件。

Method: 作者把 helper 节点下载的 trace 集合建模为一个新的码，即 repair-trace code。通过关联多项式零系数与 cyclotomic cosets 的关系，推导允许纠正 e 个错误 trace 的维度 k 上界，以及最小距离关于 k 的下界。对于 q=2，利用 cyclotomic coset 代表元的显式公式得到单错误纠正情形的精确最优维度界。同时设计两个鲁棒修复算法：一个基于 BCH 界，另一个利用字符和相关的更强界。

Result: 论文给出了纠正指定数量错误 trace 时 RS 码维度 k 的上界、repair-trace code 最小距离的下界；在 q=2 且单错误纠正时获得精确最优维度条件。提出的第一个方案达到 BCH 界保证的纠错能力，第二个方案能接近基于字符和的更强界，但计算复杂度额外增加一个 n 因子。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了低带宽 RS 码 trace 修复理论，使其具备对错误 helper 响应的鲁棒性；通过代数结构分析给出了可纠错条件和距离保证，并在纠错能力与计算复杂度之间提供了两类实用方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of robust repair of a single erasure in Reed--Solomon codes under low communication bandwidth. Focusing on the Guruswami--Wootters trace repair framework, we investigate whether a failed node can be correctly repaired in the presence of erroneous responses from helper nodes. Equivalently, we view the collection of downloaded traces as a code, which we call the repair-trace code. By characterizing the zero coefficients of the associated polynomial in terms of cyclotomic cosets, we derive upper bounds on the dimension $k$ that allow correction of a given number of erroneous traces $e$, as well as lower bounds on the minimum distance as a function of $k$. For the case $q=2$, we exploit explicit formulas for cyclotomic coset representatives to obtain the exact optimal dimension bound for single-error correction. We also propose two efficient robust repair schemes. Our first scheme achieves the error-correction capability guaranteed by the BCH bound. To approach a stronger bound based on character sums, we develop a second scheme that tolerates more errors at the cost of an additional factor $n$ in computational complexity.

</details>


### [211] [Energy Efficiency Optimization for Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Uplink NOMA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05600)
*Yixuan Li,Jun Wang,Hongbo Xu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究可旋转天线辅助的上行NOMA系统，通过联合优化接收波束成形、用户功率分配与天线旋转角度来最大化能量效率，并提出基于块坐标下降的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同时服务地面用户与空中用户的上行NOMA场景中，如何利用可旋转天线的空间自由度提升系统能量效率仍缺乏有效研究；同时，波束成形、功率控制和天线旋转之间存在强耦合，优化问题具有非凸性和求解难度。

Method: 作者构建了能量效率最大化问题，联合优化接收波束成形、用户功率分配和可旋转天线旋转。为降低求解复杂度，提出块坐标下降算法：接收波束成形采用最小均方误差准则更新；功率分配与天线旋转分别结合分式规划和逐次凸近似方法处理。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提出的RA-NOMA方案在能量效率方面优于多个基准方案，验证了可旋转天线与NOMA联合设计的性能优势。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在上行NOMA系统中引入独立可旋转天线并进行联合资源优化，可以有效提升系统能量效率；所提算法为此类非凸联合优化问题提供了可行且有效的求解框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates a rotatable antenna (RA)-enabled uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) system, where a base station equipped with multiple independently RAs serves both ground and aerial users. Specifically, we formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem by jointly optimizing receive beamforming, user power allocation, and RA rotation. To make the problem tractable, a new block coordinate descent-based algorithm is developed, in which the receive beamforming is updated via the minimum mean square error criterion, while the power allocation and RA rotation are handled by fractional programming and successive convex approximation. Numerical results demonstrate the EE superiority of the proposed RA-NOMA scheme over several benchmarks.

</details>


### [212] [Automated Proving of Shannon-Type Entropy Inequalities via Fine-Tuned Language Models and Guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05729)
*Shing Yin Wong,Shaocheng Liu,Linqi Song,Amin Gohari,Cheuk Ting Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文探索用小型微调语言模型结合引导式束搜索，自动证明 Shannon 型熵不等式；0.6B 模型在测试集上达到 85% 成功率，显著优于零样本 GPT-5.5 和 Psitip。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon 型熵不等式证明需要从已知约束中构造非平凡线性组合，本质上是随随机变量数量增长而迅速恶化的组合搜索问题，因此需要更高效的自动化证明方法。

Method: 将 0.6B--1.7B 参数的小型语言模型在原子证明步骤上微调，并与引导式 beam search 结合；在 60 个 n=10 到 15 变量的不等式测试集上评估，同时做训练上下文长度、数据分布和束搜索评分启发式的消融实验。

Result: 0.6B 微调模型结合树搜索达到 85% 证明成功率；GPT-5.5 零样本仅解出 1.7%，Psitip 解出 33.3%。4096-token 且非偏斜训练分布表现最佳；随机评分会使成功率从 83% 降至 23%。

Conclusion: 小型、领域微调的语言模型在合适搜索策略辅助下可有效自动化 Shannon 型熵不等式证明；性能关键来自训练分布匹配和有效的束搜索评分，而非更长上下文或更大通用模型。

Abstract: Proving Shannon-type entropy inequalities is a fundamental task in information theory that often requires constructing non-trivial linear combinations of known constraints, which is a combinatorial search problem that scales poorly with the number of random variables. We investigate whether small-scale large language models (0.6B--1.7B parameters), fine-tuned on atomic proof steps and combined with guided beam search, can automate this process. On a held-out test set of 60 inequalities spanning n=10 to 15 variables, our 0.6B fine-tuned model achieves an 85\% proof success rate with tree search. GPT-5.5 solves 1.7\% samples under zero-shot prompting while Psitip solves 33.3\% samples. A systematic ablation study across training context length (4096 vs.\ 8192 tokens) and data distribution (n=9-skewed vs not skewed) reveals that a 4096-token not skewed training distribution yields the best performance, with extended context and skewed data providing no marginal benefit. We further identify two dominant failure modes -- format failures and step quality degradation -- and verify that the beam-scoring heuristic is essential via a controlled ablation (random scoring reduces success from 83\% to 23\%).

</details>


### [213] [Compositional Boundaries for Density Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05871)
*Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Ali Darijani,Jürgen Beyerer,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究加权概率密度的二元融合规则在分层聚合树上执行时，何时能保持与合并顺序无关；结论是，在连续、输出权重可加且系数仅依赖权重的局部线段值融合规则中，顺序不变性刻画了归一化加权线性池化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式不确定性管理系统常因通信、隐私或调度约束沿不同聚合树合并局部概率模型。理想的最终密度应只由加权数据源决定，而不受中间节点合并顺序影响，因此需要从代数组合性角度刻画可分层执行且顺序不变的局部融合规则。

Method: 作者将二元加权概率密度融合形式化为代数组合性问题，分析局部线段值融合规则；在连续性、输出权重加性、融合系数仅依赖权重等条件下证明表征结果，并比较范数诱导的线段平衡、端点到候选的 f-散度平衡、全局散度重心以及高斯混合模型中的精确融合与逐步压缩。

Result: 顺序不变的分层执行等价于归一化加权线性池化，范数诱导线段平衡可实现相应系数；平滑端点到候选 f-散度平衡的二阶展开会产生平方根形式的有效权重，说明局部两两可解不保证调度无关；全局散度重心仍保持加性权重的局部极限；在高斯混合中，精确融合具有组合性，而逐步压缩仅在未归一化分量测度满足同余条件时具有组合性。

Conclusion: 论文明确区分了严格的顺序无关融合、全局聚合目标和局部近似启发式方法，指出只有满足特定代数结构的融合规则才能安全地用于分层分布式概率聚合。

Abstract: Distributed uncertainty-management systems often combine local probabilistic models along aggregation trees chosen by communication, privacy, or scheduling constraints. The final density should depend on the weighted sources, not on the particular order in which intermediate nodes combine them. We study this requirement as an algebraic compositionality problem for binary fusion of weighted probability densities. The central question is when a local fusion rule can be executed hierarchically while remaining order-invariant. We establish a compositional boundary for local segment-valued fusion rules. Within the class of continuous binary rules with additive output weights and weight-only coefficients, order-invariant hierarchical execution characterizes normalized weighted linear pooling; norm-induced segment balancing realizes the corresponding coefficient. Smooth endpoint-to-candidate $f$-divergence balancing has a different local geometry: its quadratic expansion induces square-root effective weights, showing why pairwise solvability alone is insufficient for schedule-independent fusion. We show that this obstruction is local to endpoint-to-candidate binary balancing, whereas global divergence barycenters retain additive-weight local limits. Finally, Gaussian mixtures show how the same issue appears in finite model classes: exact fusion is compositional, whereas stepwise compression is compositional only under a congruence condition on unnormalized component measures. These results distinguish exact schedule-independent fusion from global aggregation objectives and local approximation heuristics.

</details>


### [214] [Double-Directional Wireless Channel Modeling Using Statistics-Aided Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05993)
*Richmond Boamah,Ferdous Pervej*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种统计辅助的机器学习框架，用于生成未来双向无线信道的Top-M多径分量，实现更长期且统计一致的信道建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向无线信道模型能提供完整传播信息，对真实系统设计重要；但现有机器学习方法通常关注短时未来信道对齐，统计显著性不足，并且难以处理多径分量数量随空间、时间和交互物体变化而变化的问题。

Method: 先从完整多径分量中选择Top-M个主要分量，将其构造成可学习图；随后训练混合TimesNet-TimeFilter（TNTF）模型，并引入信道统计辅助训练，使生成的未来Top-M双向信道实现的统计特性尽可能匹配完整时变双向信道的真实统计。

Result: 在基于合成随机信道模型和确定性射线追踪的数据集上进行大量仿真验证，结果显示该方法相较现有先进基线具有更好的效果。

Conclusion: 统计辅助的固定Top-M多径选择与TNTF模型能够缓解可变多径数量和短期预测限制，在双向无线信道生成与长期统计建模中具有有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: The double-directional (DD) wireless channel model is important for realistic system design since it provides complete propagation information. While stochastic and deterministic channel models are widely adopted, and existing machine learning (ML) solutions mostly aim to align future channel realizations, these solutions are often limited to short time spans that may not be statistically significant. Moreover, because the number of multi-path components (MPCs) varies with spatial and temporal variation of the receiver (RX) and/or interacting objects (IOs), typical ML solutions that require fixed, predefined input and output shapes fall short. To curb these limitations, we propose a statistics-aided ML solution that relies on a fixed subset of MPCs selection. More specifically, we first select top-$M$ MPCs, where $M\in\mathbb{Z}^+$ is much smaller than the total number of MPCs, and construct learnable graphs to train our proposed hybrid TimesNet-TimeFilter (TNTF) model. We then use a channel statistics-aided training method to generate future top-M DD channel realizations such that the statistics calculated from these realizations matches closely with those of the actual statistics from the complete time-varying DD channel realizations. We validate the proposed solution using extensive simulations on both synthetic stochastic channel model (SCM)-based and deterministic ray-tracing-based datasets, and demonstrate its effectiveness relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [215] [Adapting Diffusion Language Models for Lossless Pixel-Level Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06273)
*Tianqi Ren,Rongpeng Li,Xianfu Chen,Yingyu Li,Zhifeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 DDM-SSCC：一种基于离散扩散模型的分离式信源-信道编码框架，用于噪声信道下的无损像素级图像传输，并在多个数据集和信道条件下优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损像素级图像传输要求精确恢复原图，既需要准确的符号概率建模以支持无损压缩，也需要在噪声信道上可靠传输。传统光栅顺序自回归编码在上下文利用和传输表示方面存在局限，因此需要更适合无损恢复与信道传输的建模方式。

Method: 作者将扩散语言模型适配到像素 token 恢复任务中，构建离散扩散模型源编码器，并通过双向注意力执行同步反向算术编码，使多个 masked token 可在一个反向去噪步骤中被编码。为弥合生成式 masked 去噪与无损算术编码之间的差距，提出 Halton 引导的去噪顺序、mask-ratio-aware 余弦调度以及轻量级温度校准模块。

Result: 在 CIFAR10、DIV2K-LR-X4 和 Kodak 数据集上，针对加性白高斯噪声和 Rayleigh 衰落信道的实验表明，DDM-SSCC 的精确恢复性能优于代表性的无损通信和语义通信基线；消融实验验证了去噪顺序、调度策略和校准模块的有效性。

Conclusion: DDM-SSCC 表明，离散扩散式渐进恢复与双向上下文建模可有效提升无损图像传输性能；结合面向算术编码的概率校准和合理去噪策略后，该框架能够在噪声信道下实现更可靠的精确图像恢复。

Abstract: Lossless pixel-level image transmission is a fundamental regime beyond semantic communications, because exact recovery requires both accurate symbol probability modeling and reliable delivery over noisy channels. This paper proposes DDM-SSCC, a discrete-diffusion-model-based separate source-channel coding framework for lossless image transmission. Different from raster-order autoregressive coding, the proposed source codec adapts a diffusion language model to pixel-token restoration and performs synchronized reverse arithmetic coding under bidirectional attention, allowing multiple masked tokens to be coded within one reverse denoising step. This progressive restoration process also yields a more favorable source representation for noisy transmission, since newly restored tokens can serve as bidirectional context in subsequent denoising steps. To bridge the gap between generation-oriented masked denoising and lossless arithmetic coding, we further introduce a Halton-guided denoising order, a mask-ratio-aware cosine schedule, and a lightweight temperature calibration module. These designs respectively improve spatial coverage, adapt the denoising pace to context reliability, and calibrate the probability tables used by arithmetic coding. Experiments on CIFAR10, DIV2K-LR-X4, and Kodak over additive white Gaussian noise and Rayleigh fading channels show that DDM-SSCC achieves better exact-recovery performance than representative lossless and semantic communication baselines, while ablation studies verify the effectiveness of the proposed denoising order, schedule, and calibration modules.

</details>


### [216] [A Spherical Stochastic Geometry Framework for Patrol-Based HAPs Network: Coverage and Energy Efficiency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06307)
*Mohammad Taha Shah,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向高空平台站（HAPs）巡逻网络的随机几何分析框架，建模、推导覆盖概率并引入覆盖能效指标，用于优化巡逻半径、密度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HAPs网络分析较少同时刻画球面地球上的周期巡逻轨迹、有限平台数量约束、干扰结构以及飞行动力学能耗。本文旨在建立能同时描述巡逻几何、通信覆盖和推进能耗的统一分析框架。

Method: 提出两类小圆环Cox过程模型：SCR-PCP中平台在局部巡逻环上服从一维泊松点过程；SCR-BCP中每个巡逻环包含固定数量的均匀分布平台。证明模型各向同性，推导最近锚点、最近巡逻环、最近HAP距离分布，以及SCR-BCP所需的联合服务距离与服务环角分布；在最近HAP关联下，将干扰分解为同环和异环部分并推导条件拉普拉斯变换；结合稳态圆周飞行推进模型定义覆盖能效CEE并分析最优巡逻半径条件。

Result: 数值结果显示，强度驱动的SCR-PCP与有限舰队式SCR-BCP部署在覆盖和干扰行为上存在本质差异；巡逻半径、平台密度和巡航速度显著影响覆盖概率与能效。

Conclusion: HAPs巡逻网络需要联合优化巡逻几何、平台部署密度/数量与飞行速度，以在覆盖性能和圆周飞行推进能耗之间取得平衡，实现能效最优运行。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic-geometry framework for high-altitude platform station (HAPs) networks in which platforms execute cyclic patrol trajectories anchored to designated service regions. We introduce two small-circle ring Cox process models on the spherical Earth. In the small-circle ring Poisson Cox process (SCR-PCP), platforms form one-dimensional Poisson point processes on localized patrol rings, whereas in the small-circle ring binomial Cox process (SCR-BCP), each ring contains a fixed number of uniformly distributed platforms. We establish the isotropy of both models and derive spatial statistics, including the distributions of the nearest-anchor, nearest-ring, and nearest-HAPs distances, together with the joint serving distance and serving ring angle distribution required for SCR-BCP analysis. Building on these results, we derive coverage probability expressions under nearest-HAPs association by decomposing aggregate interference into same-ring and other-ring components and characterizing their conditional Laplace transforms. To account for the flight dynamics of patrol-based HAPs, we integrate a steady circular flight propulsion model with the communication analysis and introduce a coverage energy efficiency (CEE) metric. This yields an analytical condition for the energy-optimal patrol radius that balances coverage performance against the propulsion cost of circular flight. Numerical results reveal fundamental differences between intensity-driven (SCR-PCP) and finite-fleet (SCR-BCP) deployments and demonstrate that patrol geometry, platform density, and cruising velocity should be jointly optimized to achieve energy-efficient HAPs operation.

</details>


### [217] [Reversible double cyclic codes over a chain ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06367)
*Mohd Anwar,Mohd Arif Raza,Mohd Rashid,Muzibur Rahman Mozumder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 R=F_q+uF_q（u^2=0）上长度为(γ,δ)的双循环码结构，给出其对偶与极小生成集，并将可逆/可逆互补条件用于构造 DNA 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双循环码具有良好的代数结构，适合用于构造纠错码；而可逆与可逆互补性质与 DNA 编码中的约束高度相关，因此有必要在有限链环 F_q+uF_q 上系统研究此类码。

Method: 作者从代数结构出发刻画长度为(γ,δ)的双循环码，分析其对偶码，给出极小张成集；进一步推导双循环码满足可逆性与可逆互补性的充要条件，并在 F_4+uF_4 上利用这些条件构造 DNA 码。

Result: 论文获得了双循环码的结构描述、对偶码性质和极小生成集；给出了可逆双循环码与可逆互补双循环码的判定条件，并构造了一些最优码作为实例支持理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了有限环上双循环码的理论，建立了其与 DNA 码构造之间的联系，为设计满足生物约束且参数较优的 DNA 码提供了代数方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of double cyclic codes of length $(γ,δ)$ over $\mathbb F_q+u\mathbb F_q, u^2=0$. We also study the dual of double cyclic code of length $(γ,δ)$ and give a minimal spanning set of double cyclic codes. Moreover, we study the necessary and sufficient conditions for a double cyclic code to be reversible and reversible-complement double cyclic code and with the help of these codes, we constructed DNA codes over $\mathbb F_4+u\mathbb F_4, u^2=0$. We also constructed some optimal codes to support our results.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了一组来自 Reddit r/ChangeMyView 的未披露 AI 账号辩论评论数据，发现这些 AI 代理系统性地采用身份塑造、权威信号、立场对齐与认知启发式触发来提升说服效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未披露的 AI 账号在真实在线审议论坛中参与辩论，会模糊真实与合成主体的认知可信度边界。该研究希望利用一次罕见公开数据，理解大语言模型在身份丰富、互动性强的公共讨论环境中如何构建说服力与可信度。

Method: 研究对公开释放的 AI 生成评论语料进行结构化内容分析，考察身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略以及认知启发式激活；并将其与人类撰写的 r/ChangeMyView 反驳评论进行对比。

Result: 超过三分之二的 AI 评论出现身份定位或身份采纳；几乎所有评论都包含对齐动作和权威主张；大多数评论触发确认偏误、代表性启发和可得性启发等认知机制。与人类评论相比，AI 评论在各维度上呈现相反分布：权威使用更密集，对齐更具对抗性，更依赖外部引用而非个人经验。

Conclusion: 这些 AI 代理形成了一种以说服效率而非真实审议参与为目标的修辞架构。单纯要求披露 AI 身份不足以解决合成主体带来的可信度不对称问题；未来需要能够审计 AI 如何组织、制造和呈现可信度的评估框架。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [219] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PACT，将多智能体系统中的自由文本通信压缩为结构化的动作-状态记录，以降低 token 消耗并改善性能-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常关注角色、流程和轮次调度，但智能体间传递内容多为不受约束的自然语言，容易导致 token 膨胀、占用共享上下文窗口，并增加推理成本、影响系统性能。

Method: 作者分析了两类 MAS 拓扑中的五种常见智能体间通信策略，发现固定策略并非普遍最优；进一步提出 PACT，将智能体通信建模为公共状态更新问题，把原始输出投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录后再写入共享历史。

Result: PACT 在不同 MAS 拓扑中持续改善性能-成本权衡，以显著更少的 token 达到相当或更强的任务表现；在生产级编码框架中，PACT 使 OpenHands 的 resolve rate 提升且每解决任务 token 减少约 10%，在 SWE-agent 上保持 resolve-neutral 同时将输入 token 减半。

Conclusion: 高效的智能体间通信应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息，而非依赖自由文本或固定通信策略；PACT 通过协议化、结构化的动作-状态传输，为 LLM 多智能体系统提供了更高效且可扩展的通信机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [220] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Query Retrieve Conclude（QRC）零样本框架，用于为多模态梗图自动识别缺失背景知识、检索开放网络证据，并生成有依据的背景知识，从而提升梗图理解与检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
